## `ZAF-4741-OPS-R2-OPS-SC-02`

- **Country:** `ZAF` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Ultrasonic-Bath Mechanic
- **Scenario:** In-situ Removal, Reassembly & Dynamic Tolerance Reset SOP for Device Security Display Stand & DC Power Bus Wear Components

Insulation resistance to earth must be > 1 MΩ at 500 VDC.
